# Third-Party Software Notices

Tir-na Nog'th is built with the Rust ecosystem. The libraries below are compiled into the
game binary and are used under the licenses shown. This file reproduces the full text of each
license, with the crates it covers. Font and 3D-asset attributions are in `CREDITS.md`.

Total: 512 crates.
